These findings highlight a fundamental limitation of current unlearning methods: without accounting for embedding geometry, they risk amplifying bias in retained groups.

</details>


### [147] [Alloc-MoE: Budget-Aware Expert Activation Allocation for Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08133)
*Baihui Liu,Kaiyuan Tian,Wei Wang,Zhaoning Zhang,Linbo Qiao,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Alloc-MoE：一种在层和token级别协调优化专家激活分配的框架，在约束激活预算下保持MoE模型性能，同时提升推理速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构中大量专家激活在推理时造成关键延迟瓶颈，特别是在资源受限场景。现有减少专家激活的方法可能导致严重的模型性能下降，需要在约束激活预算下最小化性能损失。

Method: 提出Alloc-MoE统一框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Alloc-L（层级分配）：通过敏感性分析和动态规划确定跨层的最优专家激活分配；2) Alloc-T（token级分配）：基于路由分数动态重新分配激活，优化预算分配而不增加延迟。

Result: 在多个MoE模型上的实验表明，Alloc-MoE能在约束激活预算下保持模型性能。特别是在DeepSeek-V2-Lite上，以原始预算的一半实现了1.15倍预填充和1.34倍解码加速。

Conclusion: Alloc-MoE通过协调的层和token级预算分配，有效解决了MoE模型推理延迟瓶颈，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理速度，为资源受限部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a dominant architecture for scaling large language models due to their sparse activation mechanism. However, the substantial number of expert activations creates a critical latency bottleneck during inference, especially in resource-constrained deployment scenarios. Existing approaches that reduce expert activations potentially lead to severe model performance degradation. In this work, we introduce the concept of \emph{activation budget} as a constraint on the number of expert activations and propose Alloc-MoE, a unified framework that optimizes budget allocation coordinately at both the layer and token levels to minimize performance degradation. At the layer level, we introduce Alloc-L, which leverages sensitivity profiling and dynamic programming to determine the optimal allocation of expert activations across layers. At the token level, we propose Alloc-T, which dynamically redistributes activations based on routing scores, optimizing budget allocation without increasing latency. Extensive experiments across multiple MoE models demonstrate that Alloc-MoE maintains model performance under a constrained activation budget. Especially, Alloc-MoE achieves $1.15\times$ prefill and $1.34\times$ decode speedups on DeepSeek-V2-Lite at half of the original budget.

</details>


### [148] [A Direct Approach for Handling Contextual Bandits with Latent State Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08149)
*Zhen Li,Gilles Stoltz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Nelson等人(2022)提出的有限臂线性bandit模型，改进了其简化假设，建立了更自然、更强大的高概率遗憾界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nelson等人(2022)的模型存在三个主要问题：1) 对奖励函数进行了简化假设（基于后验概率而非隐藏状态本身）；2) 分析未考虑HMM参数估计；3) 只获得期望遗憾界而非高概率界，且依赖不必要的复杂模型参数

Method: 研究更自然的模型，其中奖励直接依赖于隐藏状态（同时保留对观测上下文的依赖）。开发完全自适应的在线策略，实时估计HMM参数，并获得高概率遗憾界

Result: 获得了更强的、高概率的遗憾界，这些界限不依赖于奖励函数，仅通过HMM参数估计依赖于模型，避免了不必要的复杂模型依赖

Conclusion: 本文提出了更自然的有限臂线性bandit模型，克服了先前工作的简化假设和局限性，通过完全自适应策略和在线参数估计获得了更强大、更简洁的遗憾界

Abstract: We revisit the finite-armed linear bandit model by Nelson et al. (2022), where contexts and rewards are governed by a finite hidden Markov chain. Nelson et al. (2022) approach this model by a reduction to linear contextual bandits; but to do so, they actually introduce a simplification in which rewards are linear functions of the posterior probabilities over the hidden states given the observed contexts, rather than functions of the hidden states themselves. Their analysis (but not their algorithm) also does not take into account the estimation of the HMM parameters, and only tackles expected, not high-probability, bounds, which suffer in addition from unnecessary complex dependencies on the model (like reward gaps). We instead study the more natural model incorporating direct dependencies in the hidden states (on top of dependencies on the observed contexts, as is natural for contextual bandits) and also obtain stronger, high-probability, regret bounds for a fully adaptive strategy that estimates HMM parameters online. These bounds do not depend on the reward functions and only depend on the model through the estimation of the HMM parameters.

</details>


### [149] [Shift- and stretch-invariant non-negative matrix factorization with an application to brain tissue delineation in emission tomography data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08161)
*Anders S. Olsen,Miriam L. Navarro,Claus Svarer,Jesper L. Hinrich,Morten Mørup,Gitte M. Knudsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出shift- and stretch-invariant非负矩阵分解框架，用于处理动态神经影像数据中的扩散效应，通过频域方法估计时间偏移和拉伸，提高脑组织结构表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态神经影像数据（如放射性示踪剂传输的发射断层扫描）常表现出扩散特性，导致距离依赖的时间延迟、尺度差异和拉伸效应，限制了传统线性建模和分解方法的有效性。

Method: 提出shift- and stretch-invariant非负矩阵分解框架，在频域中同时估计整数和非整数时间偏移以及时间拉伸。偏移通过相位修正处理，拉伸通过零填充或截断实现。模型基于PyTorch实现。

Result: 在合成数据和脑发射断层扫描数据上验证，模型能够通过考虑拉伸效应，提供更详细的脑组织结构表征。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了动态神经影像数据中的扩散相关效应，提高了分解方法对时间延迟和尺度变化的鲁棒性，为脑组织分析提供了更准确的工具。

Abstract: Dynamic neuroimaging data, such as emission tomography measurements of radiotracer transport in blood or cerebrospinal fluid, often exhibit diffusion-like properties. These introduce distance-dependent temporal delays, scale-differences, and stretching effects that limit the effectiveness of conventional linear modeling and decomposition methods. To address this, we present the shift- and stretch-invariant non-negative matrix factorization framework. Our approach estimates both integer and non-integer temporal shifts as well as temporal stretching, all implemented in the frequency domain, where shifts correspond to phase modifications, and where stretching is handled via zero-padding or truncation. The model is implemented in PyTorch (https://github.com/anders-s-olsen/shiftstretchNMF). We demonstrate on synthetic data and brain emission tomography data that the model is able to account for stretching to provide more detailed characterization of brain tissue structure.

</details>


### [150] [Value-Guidance MeanFlow for Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08174)
*Teng Pang,Zhiqiang Dong,Yan Zhang,Rongjian Xu,Guoqiang Wu,Yilong Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VGM²P：基于价值引导的多智能体均值流策略框架，通过条件行为克隆实现高效离线多智能体强化学习，避免了对行为正则化系数的敏感依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多智能体强化学习需要在最大化全局回报和缓解离线数据分布偏移之间进行权衡。现有方法通常使用扩散或流生成模型，但依赖多步迭代采样导致训练和推理效率低下。虽然蒸馏等方法能提高采样效率，但对行为正则化系数敏感。

Method: 提出VGM²P框架：1）使用全局优势值引导智能体协作，将最优策略学习视为条件行为克隆；2）利用无分类器引导的均值流（MeanFlow）提高策略表达能力和推理效率；3）实现系数不敏感的条件行为克隆。

Result: 在离散和连续动作空间任务上的实验表明，仅通过条件行为克隆训练，VGM²P就能高效达到与最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: VGM²P是一个简单有效的流基策略学习框架，通过价值引导和均值流技术，实现了高效的动作生成和系数不敏感的条件行为克隆，在离线多智能体强化学习中表现出色。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) aims to learn the optimal joint policy from pre-collected datasets, requiring a trade-off between maximizing global returns and mitigating distribution shift from offline data. Recent studies use diffusion or flow generative models to capture complex joint policy behaviors among agents; however, they typically rely on multi-step iterative sampling, thereby reducing training and inference efficiency. Although further research improves sampling efficiency through methods like distillation, it remains sensitive to the behavior regularization coefficient. To address the above-mentioned issues, we propose Value Guidance Multi-agent MeanFlow Policy (VGM$^2$P), a simple yet effective flow-based policy learning framework that enables efficient action generation with coefficient-insensitive conditional behavior cloning. Specifically, VGM$^2$P uses global advantage values to guide agent collaboration, treating optimal policy learning as conditional behavior cloning. Additionally, to improve policy expressiveness and inference efficiency in multi-agent scenarios, it leverages classifier-free guidance MeanFlow for both policy training and execution. Experiments on tasks with both discrete and continuous action spaces demonstrate that, even when trained solely via conditional behavior cloning, VGM$^2$P efficiently achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [151] [Long-Term Embeddings for Balanced Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08181)
*Andrii Dzhoha,Egor Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Long-Term Embeddings (LTE)框架解决序列推荐中的近期偏差问题，通过基于内容的固定语义基础确保跨版本兼容性，解决生产环境中的时间点一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于Transformer的序列推荐器擅长捕捉短期意图但存在近期偏差，忽略稳定的长期偏好。扩展序列长度计算效率低，且近期交互主导注意力。生产环境中存在时间点一致性问题，特征存储通常只保存特征的"实时"版本，导致模型部署和回滚时出现离线-在线不匹配。

Method: 提出LTE框架，将嵌入约束到基于内容的项目表示的固定语义基础上，确保跨版本兼容性。研究因果语言建模的集成策略，考虑LTE和Transformer短期序列共享时间范围时的数据泄漏问题。评估两种表示：启发式平均和具有固定解码器的不对称自编码器，该解码器基于语义基础，可在保持稳定性的同时进行行为微调。

Result: 在Zalando的在线A/B测试中，使用滞后窗口将LTE作为上下文前缀令牌集成，在用户参与度和财务指标方面均获得显著提升。

Conclusion: LTE框架有效解决了序列推荐中的长期偏好建模问题，通过基于内容的固定语义基础确保了生产环境中的跨版本兼容性，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern transformer-based sequential recommenders excel at capturing short-term intent but often suffer from recency bias, overlooking stable long-term preferences. While extending sequence lengths is an intuitive fix, it is computationally inefficient, and recent interactions tend to dominate the model's attention. We propose Long-Term Embeddings (LTE) as a high-inertia contextual anchor to bridge this gap. We address a critical production challenge: the point-in-time consistency problem caused by infrastructure constraints, as feature stores typically host only a single "live" version of features. This leads to an offline-online mismatch during model deployments and rollbacks, as models are forced to process evolved representations they never saw during training. To resolve this, we introduce an LTE framework that constrains embeddings to a fixed semantic basis of content-based item representations, ensuring cross-version compatibility. Furthermore, we investigate integration strategies for causal language modeling, considering the data leakage issue that occurs when the LTE and the transformer's short-term sequence share a temporal horizon. We evaluate two representations: a heuristic average and an asymmetric autoencoder with a fixed decoder grounded in the semantic basis to enable behavioral fine-tuning while maintaining stability. Online A/B tests on Zalando demonstrate that integrating LTE as a contextual prefix token using a lagged window yields significant uplifts in both user engagement and financial metrics.

</details>


### [152] [Equivariant Efficient Joint Discrete and Continuous MeanFlow for Molecular Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08189)
*Rongjian Xu,Teng Pang,Zhiqiang Dong,Guoqiang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EQUIMF是一种统一的SE(3)-等变生成框架，通过同步的MeanFlow动力学联合建模离散拓扑和连续几何，实现高效少步采样并保持物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图生成方法通常将结构与几何解耦，缺乏同步的跨域动力学，依赖迭代采样，导致物理不一致的分子构象和采样速度慢。需要统一的等变框架来联合建模离散和连续组件。

Method: 提出EQUIMF框架，引入统一时间桥和平均速度更新，通过相互条件在结构与几何之间实现同步MeanFlow动力学。开发了新颖的离散MeanFlow公式，支持离散图结构的高效生成。

Result: EQUIMF在生成质量、物理有效性和采样效率方面一致优于先前的扩散和流匹配方法。

Conclusion: EQUIMF通过同步的MeanFlow动力学统一建模离散和连续组件，解决了图结构数据生成中的关键挑战，实现了高效且物理一致的生成。

Abstract: Graph-structured data jointly contain discrete topology and continuous geometry, which poses fundamental challenges for generative modeling due to heterogeneous distributions, incompatible noise dynamics, and the need for equivariant inductive biases. Existing flow-matching approaches for graph generation typically decouple structure from geometry, lack synchronized cross-domain dynamics, and rely on iterative sampling, often resulting in physically inconsistent molecular conformations and slow sampling. To address these limitations, we propose Equivariant MeanFlow (EQUIMF), a unified SE(3)-equivariant generative framework that jointly models discrete and continuous components through synchronized MeanFlow dynamics. EQUIMF introduces a unified time bridge and average-velocity updates with mutual conditioning between structure and geometry, enabling efficient few-step generation while preserving physical consistency. Moreover, we develop a novel discrete MeanFlow formulation with a simple yet effective parameterization to support efficient generation over discrete graph structures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EQUIMF consistently outperforms prior diffusion and flow-matching methods in generation quality, physical validity, and sampling efficiency.

</details>


### [153] [An Illusion of Unlearning? Assessing Machine Unlearning Through Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08271)
*Yichen Gao,Altay Unal,Akshay Rangamani,Zhihui Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示当前机器遗忘方法主要依赖特征-分类器错位而非真正特征遗忘，提出基于类均值特征的分类器方法实现真正的表示层遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法虽然在输出层面表现良好，但存在脆弱性（如微调即可恢复遗忘概念），且缺乏对内部表示层面的分析。作者旨在揭示当前方法成功表象下的真实机制，并提出更可靠的遗忘方法。

Method: 1) 分析遗忘模型的内部表示，发现特征-分类器错位现象；2) 基于神经坍缩假设，证明仅调整分类器即可实现遗忘；3) 提出基于类均值特征分类器的遗忘方法，强制特征与分类器对齐；4) 在标准基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明：1) 现有SOTA遗忘方法主要依赖特征-分类器错位，隐藏特征仍高度可区分；2) 简单线性探测可恢复接近原始准确率；3) 仅分类器微调即可实现低遗忘准确率同时保持保留准确率；4) CMF-based遗忘方法能有效减少表示中的遗忘信息，同时保持高保留准确率。

Conclusion: 当前机器遗忘方法评估过于依赖输出层面，需要更忠实的表示层面评估。基于类均值特征的分类器方法能实现真正的表示层遗忘，为机器遗忘研究提供了新的方向和评估标准。

Abstract: While numerous machine unlearning (MU) methods have recently been developed with promising results in erasing the influence of forgotten data, classes, or concepts, they are also highly vulnerable-for example, simple fine-tuning can inadvertently reintroduce erased concepts. In this paper, we address this contradiction by examining the internal representations of unlearned models, in contrast to prior work that focuses primarily on output-level behavior. Our analysis shows that many state-of-the-art MU methods appear successful mainly due to a misalignment between last-layer features and the classifier, a phenomenon we call feature-classifier misalignment. In fact, hidden features remain highly discriminative, and simple linear probing can recover near-original accuracy. Assuming neural collapse in the original model, we further demonstrate that adjusting only the classifier can achieve negligible forget accuracy while preserving retain accuracy, and we corroborate this with experiments using classifier-only fine-tuning. Motivated by these findings, we propose MU methods based on a class-mean features (CMF) classifier, which explicitly enforces alignment between features and classifiers. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CMF-based unlearning reduces forgotten information in representations while maintaining high retain accuracy, highlighting the need for faithful representation-level evaluation of MU.

</details>


### [154] [Dead Weights, Live Signals: Feedforward Graphs of Frozen Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08335)
*Marcus Armstrong,Navid Ayoobi,Arjun Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于冻结大语言模型的图架构，通过共享潜在空间和线性投影实现异构模型间的通信，仅训练少量参数即可显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用异构大语言模型的互补能力，通过共享潜在空间实现模型间的协同推理，同时保持大部分参数冻结以降低计算成本。

Method: 构建前馈图架构，三个小型冻结模型编码输入到共享潜在空间，通过学习的线性投影将聚合信号注入两个大型冻结模型，最后通过轻量级交叉注意力输出节点生成结果。

Result: 仅训练1760万参数（相对于约120亿冻结参数），在ARC-Challenge、OpenBookQA和MMLU上分别达到87.3%、82.8%和67.2%，显著优于单个组成模型和参数匹配的基线方法。

Conclusion: 异构冻结大语言模型通过共享潜在空间和可训练的投影矩阵可以形成有效的协同推理系统，梯度在多模型边界间的流动是可行的，输出节点能自发学习选择性路由行为。

Abstract: We present a feedforward graph architecture in which heterogeneous frozen large language models serve as computational nodes, communicating through a shared continuous latent space via learned linear projections. Building on recent work demonstrating geometric compatibility between independently trained LLM latent spaces~\cite{armstrong2026thinking}, we extend this finding from static two-model steering to end-to-end trainable multi-node graphs, where projection matrices are optimized jointly via backpropagation through residual stream injection hooks. Three small frozen models (Llama-3.2-1B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, Gemma-2-2B) encode the input into a shared latent space whose aggregate signal is injected into two larger frozen models (Phi-3-mini, Mistral-7B), whose representations feed a lightweight cross-attention output node. With only 17.6M trainable parameters against approximately 12B frozen, the architecture achieves 87.3\% on ARC-Challenge, 82.8\% on OpenBookQA, and 67.2\% on MMLU, outperforming the best single constituent model by 11.4, 6.2, and 1.2 percentage points respectively, and outperforming parameter-matched learned classifiers on frozen single models by 9.1, 5.2, and 6.7 points. Gradient flow through multiple frozen model boundaries is empirically verified to be tractable, and the output node develops selective routing behavior across layer-2 nodes without explicit supervision.

</details>


### [155] [Leveraging Complementary Embeddings for Replay Selection in Continual Learning with Small Buffers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08336)
*Danit Yanowsky,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MERS：一种基于多嵌入重放选择的持续学习方法，通过整合监督和无监督嵌入来改进重放缓冲区样本选择，在低内存条件下显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题仍然是一个关键挑战。在内存受限的重放式持续学习中，性能严重依赖于重放缓冲区的样本选择策略。现有方法主要使用监督学习得到的嵌入来构建内存缓冲区，但忽视了无监督表示中丰富的类别相关语义信息。

Method: 提出多嵌入重放选择方法MERS，用基于图的方法替换缓冲区选择模块，该方法整合了监督和无监督嵌入。该方法不增加模型参数或重放数据量，可作为即插即用的增强模块用于重放式持续学习算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上的实验结果表明，MERS在多种持续学习算法中一致优于最先进的样本选择策略，特别是在低内存条件下表现出更强的性能提升。

Conclusion: MERS通过整合监督和无监督嵌入，提供了一种实用且有效的重放缓冲区选择策略，显著改善了持续学习在内存受限条件下的性能，为解决灾难性遗忘问题提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a key challenge in Continual Learning (CL). In replay-based CL with severe memory constraints, performance critically depends on the sample selection strategy for the replay buffer. Most existing approaches construct memory buffers using embeddings learned under supervised objectives. However, class-agnostic, self-supervised representations often encode rich, class-relevant semantics that are overlooked. We propose a new method, Multiple Embedding Replay Selection, MERS, which replaces the buffer selection module with a graph-based approach that integrates both supervised and self-supervised embeddings. Empirical results show consistent improvements over SOTA selection strategies across a range of continual learning algorithms, with particularly strong gains in low-memory regimes. On CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet, MERS outperforms single-embedding baselines without adding model parameters or increasing replay volume, making it a practical, drop-in enhancement for replay-based continual learning.

</details>


### [156] [EgoEverything: A Benchmark for Human Behavior Inspired Long Context Egocentric Video Understanding in AR Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08342)
*Qiance Tang,Ziqi Wang,Jieyu Lin,Ziyun Li,Barbara De Salvo,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EgoEverything是一个用于长上下文第一人称视频理解的基准测试，通过整合人类注意力信号来生成问题，包含5000多个多项选择题对，覆盖100多小时视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称视频数据集主要依赖人类佩戴的摄像头，侧重于视觉内容，对形成视频相关查询时用户底层行为的考虑有限。增强现实(AR)作为重要应用领域，需要能够推理长时间上下文和多样化非结构化活动的基准测试。

Method: 通过利用从注视数据中抽象出的人类注意力信号来生成问题，创建了一个包含5000多个多项选择题对的基准测试，覆盖100多小时的第一人称视频，更真实地捕捉自然人类行为。

Result: 构建了EgoEverything基准测试，包含5000多个多项选择题对，覆盖100多小时视频，通过整合人类注意力信号提供更真实的评估设置。

Conclusion: EgoEverything通过整合人类注意力信号生成问题，为AR中的长上下文第一人称视频理解提供了更真实、基于人类行为的评估基准。

Abstract: Long context egocentric video understanding has recently attracted significant research attention, with augmented reality (AR) highlighted as one of its most important application domains. Nevertheless, the task remains highly challenging due to the need for reasoning over extended temporal contexts and diverse, unstructured activities. Although several benchmarks exist, most egocentric datasets rely on human worn cameras and focus mainly on visual content, with limited consideration of underlying user behavior when forming video-related queries. EgoEverything is a benchmark that explicitly considers human behavior by leveraging human attention signals, abstracted from gaze data, when generating questions. It comprises over 5,000 multiple choice question answer pairs, spanning more than 100 hours of video. By integrating human attention signals during question generation, it more faithfully captures natural human behavior and offers a realistic evaluation setting for long-context egocentric video understanding in AR.

</details>


### [157] [Scaling-Aware Data Selection for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08366)
*Tolga Dimlioglu,Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一种数据选择框架，通过分区、拟合神经缩放定律和迭代优化数据混合，在自动驾驶规划任务中比基线方法用少80%的数据获得更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模深度学习模型需要多样化的训练数据，但现有数据选择框架无法处理数据点对不同评估指标影响的模糊性，特别是在需要满足多种部署标准的物理AI应用中。

Method: MOSAIC框架包含三个步骤：1) 将数据集划分为不同域；2) 为每个数据域拟合评估指标的神经缩放定律；3) 通过迭代添加能最大化指标变化的数据域来优化数据混合。

Result: 在自动驾驶端到端规划器模型中，MOSAIC在扩展预测驾驶员模型评分（EPDMS）上优于多种基线方法，且使用数据量减少高达80%。

Conclusion: MOSAIC提供了一个通用的数据选择框架，能够有效优化训练数据混合，在保持性能的同时显著减少所需数据量，特别适用于需要满足多种评估标准的物理AI应用。

Abstract: Large-scale deep learning models for physical AI applications depend on diverse training data collection efforts. These models and correspondingly, the training data, must address different evaluation criteria necessary for the models to be deployable in real-world environments. Data selection policies can guide the development of the training set, but current frameworks do not account for the ambiguity in how data points affect different metrics. In this work, we propose Mixture Optimization via Scaling-Aware Iterative Collection (MOSAIC), a general data selection framework that operates by: (i) partitioning the dataset into domains; (ii) fitting neural scaling laws from each data domain to the evaluation metrics; and (iii) optimizing a data mixture by iteratively adding data from domains that maximize the change in metrics. We apply MOSAIC to autonomous driving (AD), where an End-to-End (E2E) planner model is evaluated on the Extended Predictive Driver Model Score (EPDMS), an aggregate of driving rule compliance metrics. Here, MOSAIC outperforms a diverse set of baselines on EPDMS with up to 80\% less data.

</details>


### [158] [SOLAR: Communication-Efficient Model Adaptation via Subspace-Oriented Latent Adapter Reparametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08368)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi,Xiaolong Ma,Lei Yang,Feng Yan,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR是一种后训练压缩框架，通过将PEFT适配器表示为基向量的线性组合，显著降低通信和存储成本，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）虽然能有效适应基础模型，但在资源受限环境中的通信和存储成本仍然是主要瓶颈，需要更高效的压缩方案。

Method: SOLAR将每个PEFT更新表示为基向量的线性组合，这些基向量来自基础模型的奇异向量加上受控随机扰动。利用基础模型与任务特定微调更新之间的子空间相似性，将适配器大小与PEFT结构解耦，确保紧凑且富有表现力的表示。

Result: 在语言和视觉任务上使用LLaMA、GPT和ViT模型的实验表明，SOLAR在显著减少模型表示大小的同时保持了任务性能，为分布式系统和边缘设备部署提供了有效的通信高效解决方案。

Conclusion: SOLAR是一个模型无关且与现有PEFT方法兼容的压缩框架，通过利用子空间相似性实现了PEFT适配器的高效压缩，在保持性能的同时大幅降低了通信和存储成本。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as LoRA, enable scalable adaptation of foundation models by injecting low-rank adapters. However, their communication and storage costs remain a major bottleneck in resource-constrained settings. We propose SOLAR (Subspace-Oriented Latent Adapter Reparameterization), a post-training compression framework that substantially reduces the communication cost (i.e., the number of parameters to transmit or store) of PEFT adapters. SOLAR expresses each PEFT update as a linear combination of basis vectors formed from the foundation model's singular vectors with controlled random perturbations. By exploiting the subspace similarity (the alignment of principal directions) between the foundation model and task-specific fine-tuned updates, SOLAR decouples the adapter size from PEFT structure and ensures compact yet expressive representations. It is model-agnostic and compatible with existing PEFT methods, including LoRA, AdaLoRA, and other adapter modules. We theoretically establish a bound on the reconstruction error. Experiments on language and vision tasks using LLaMA, GPT, and ViT models demonstrate that SOLAR preserves task performance while significantly reducing model representation sizes, offering an effective and communication-efficient solution for deployment in distributed systems and edge devices.

</details>


### [159] [ADAPTive Input Training for Many-to-One Pre-Training on Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08398)
*Paul Quinlan,Qingguo Li,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT提出了一种新的时间序列预训练范式，能够有效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，支持在输入尺寸和通道维度差异极大的数据集上进行混合批次预训练，为构建时间序列基础模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列自监督预训练方法在单一数据集预训练-下游任务微调的一对多场景中表现良好，但在预训练阶段加入多个数据集时难以泛化到新数据集。这限制了利用大量多样化数据集构建时间序列基础模型的能力。

Method: ADAPT预训练范式能够有效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，支持在输入尺寸和通道维度存在极端差异的数据集上进行混合批次预训练。该方法在162个时间序列分类数据集上进行训练。

Result: ADAPT在分类基准测试中取得了新的最先进性能，成功实现了在时间序列领域对广泛数据集的同时训练，这是构建时间序列领域通用基础模型的重要基础。

Conclusion: ADAPT解决了时间序列预训练中多数据集泛化的基本挑战，为构建能够从多样化数据集中学习的时间序列基础模型提供了关键技术支持。

Abstract: Recent work on time-series models has leveraged self-supervised training to learn meaningful features and patterns in order to improve performance on downstream tasks and generalize to unseen modalities. While these pretraining methods have shown great promise in one-to-many scenarios, where a model is pre-trained on one dataset and fine-tuned on a downstream dataset, they have struggled to generalize to new datasets when more datasets are added during pre-training. This is a fundamental challenge in building foundation models for time-series data, as it limits the ability to develop models that can learn from a large variety of diverse datasets available. To address this challenge, we present a new pre-training paradigm for time-series data called ADAPT, which can efficiently align the physical properties of data in the time-series domain, enabling mixed-batch pre-training despite the extreme discrepancies in the input sizes and channel dimensions of pre-training data. We trained on 162 time-series classification datasets and set new state-of-the-art performance for classification benchmarks. We successfully train a model within the time-series domain on a wide range of datasets simultaneously, which is a major building block for building generalist foundation models in time-series domains.

</details>


### [160] [Adversarial Label Invariant Graph Data Augmentations for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08404)
*Simon Zhang,Ryan P. DeMilt,Kun Jin,Cathy H. Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIA方法，通过对抗性训练实现协变量偏移下的分布外泛化，利用对抗性标签不变数据增强探索训练数据环境，防止模型坍缩到分布内训练的学习器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实应用中，训练和测试数据常来自不同环境，导致分布偏移。协变量偏移是一种仅发生在输入数据而概念分布保持不变的分布偏移类型。现有方法在处理此类分布外泛化问题时存在局限性，需要新的正则化方法来提高模型在协变量偏移下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出RIA（Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training）方法，受Q学习启发，通过对抗性探索训练数据环境。使用对抗性标签不变数据增强诱导新环境，防止模型坍缩到分布内训练的学习器。该方法可与许多现有的协变量偏移分布外泛化方法结合，这些方法可表述为约束优化问题。开发了交替梯度下降-上升算法来解决问题。

Result: 在分布外图分类任务上进行了广泛实验，涵盖各种合成和自然分布偏移。实验结果表明，与现有分布外基线方法相比，RIA方法能够达到更高的准确率。

Conclusion: RIA方法通过对抗性训练和正则化机制，有效提升了模型在协变量偏移下的分布外泛化能力，为处理训练和测试数据分布不一致的实际问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OoD) generalization occurs when representation learning encounters a distribution shift. This occurs frequently in practice when training and testing data come from different environments. Covariate shift is a type of distribution shift that occurs only in the input data, while the concept distribution stays invariant. We propose RIA - Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training, a new method for OoD generalization under convariate shift. Motivated by an analogy to $Q$-learning, it performs an adversarial exploration for training data environments. These new environments are induced by adversarial label invariant data augmentations that prevent a collapse to an in-distribution trained learner. It works with many existing OoD generalization methods for covariate shift that can be formulated as constrained optimization problems. We develop an alternating gradient descent-ascent algorithm to solve the problem, and perform extensive experiments on OoD graph classification for various kinds of synthetic and natural distribution shifts. We demonstrate that our method can achieve high accuracy compared with OoD baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Provably Adaptive Linear Approximation for the Shapley Value and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08438)
*Weida Li,Yaoliang Yu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Adalina算法，在Θ(n)空间约束下高效近似半值，实现O(n/ε² log(1/δ))查询复杂度，改进了均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值及其半值家族在归因问题中广泛应用，但精确计算需要指数级查询。大规模应用需要高效近似算法，特别是在Θ(n)空间约束下。

Method: 基于向量集中不等式建立理论框架，系统开发线性空间算法。框架统一了OFA、无偏kernelSHAP、SHAP-IQ和回归调整方法，引入自适应线性时间线性空间随机算法Adalina。

Result: 实现了O(n/ε² log(1/δ))查询复杂度保证P(‖φ̂-φ‖₂≥ε)≤δ，理论上改进了均方误差，并通过实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 在Θ(n)空间约束下建立了半值近似的最优查询复杂度界限，提出的Adalina算法在理论和实验上均表现出色，为大规模归因问题提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The Shapley value, and its broader family of semi-values, has received much attention in various attribution problems. A fundamental and long-standing challenge is their efficient approximation, since exact computation generally requires an exponential number of utility queries in the number of players $n$. To meet the challenges of large-scale applications, we explore the limits of efficiently approximating semi-values under a $Θ(n)$ space constraint. Building upon a vector concentration inequality, we establish a theoretical framework that enables sharper query complexities for existing unbiased randomized algorithms. Within this framework, we systematically develop a linear-space algorithm that requires $O(\frac{n}{ε^{2}}\log\frac{1}δ)$ utility queries to ensure $P(\|\hat{\boldsymbolφ}-\boldsymbolφ\|_{2}\geqε)\leq δ$ for all commonly used semi-values. In particular, our framework naturally bridges OFA, unbiased kernelSHAP, SHAP-IQ and the regression-adjusted approach, and definitively characterizes when paired sampling is beneficial. Moreover, our algorithm allows explicit minimization of the mean square error for each specific utility function. Accordingly, we introduce the first adaptive, linear-time, linear-space randomized algorithm, Adalina, that theoretically achieves improved mean square error. All of our theoretical findings are experimentally validated.

</details>


### [162] [Less Approximates More: Harmonizing Performance and Confidence Faithfulness via Hybrid Post-Training for High-Stakes Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08454)
*Haokai Ma,Lee Yan Zhen,Gang Yang,Yunshan Ma,Ee-Chien Chang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyTuning：一种混合后训练框架，通过渐进推理增益度量自适应重加权推理蒸馏和内部反馈强化学习，在有限监督下提升准确性和置信度忠实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在关键任务中的部署使得置信度忠实性问题变得重要，现有方法面临高质量训练语料稀缺、事实性过度自信和错误更新放大等挑战

Method: 提出渐进推理增益度量推理步骤是否逐步支持最终答案，并设计HyTuning混合后训练框架，通过PRG风格度量自适应重加权推理蒸馏和RLIF，利用少量监督推理轨迹作为稳定锚点，同时利用大量未标注查询实现可扩展性

Result: 在多个领域特定和通用基准测试中，HyTuning在有限监督下提高了准确性并实现了置信度忠实性，支持"少近似多"的实际效果

Conclusion: HyTuning通过渐进推理增益度量和自适应混合训练策略，有效解决了语言模型置信度忠实性问题，在有限监督资源下实现了性能提升

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes tasks, where confident yet incorrect inferences may cause severe real-world harm, bringing the previously overlooked issue of confidence faithfulness back to the forefront. A promising solution is to jointly optimize unsupervised Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) with reasoning-trace-guided Reasoning Distillation (RD), which may face three persistent challenges: scarcity of high-quality training corpora, factually unwarranted overconfidence and indiscriminate fusion that amplifies erroneous updates. Inspired by the human confidence accumulation from uncertainty to certainty, we propose Progressive Reasoning Gain (PRG) to measure whether reasoning steps progressively strengthen support for the final answer. Furthermore, we introduce HyTuning, a hybrid post-training framework that adaptively reweights RD and RLIF via a PRG-style metric, using scarce supervised reasoning traces as a stable anchor while exploiting abundant unlabeled queries for scalability. Experiments on several domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that HyTuning improves accuracy while achieving confidence faithfulness under limited supervision, supporting a practical "Less Approximates More" effect.

</details>


### [163] [A Machine Learning Framework for Turbofan Health Estimation via Inverse Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08460)
*Milad Leyli-Abadi,Lucas Thil,Sebastien Razakarivony,Guillaume Doquet,Jesse Read*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种从涡轮风扇发动机传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标的新方法，引入包含维护事件和使用变化的新数据集，比较稳态与非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器以及自监督学习方法，为健康估计问题建立实用基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计是一个具有挑战性的不适定逆问题，受限于稀疏传感和复杂非线性热力学。现有研究存在碎片化问题，使用不现实数据集且对时间信息利用不足。需要在实际退化和维护模式下从操作传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标。

Method: 1) 引入包含行业导向复杂性（维护事件和使用变化）的新数据集；2) 建立基准比较稳态与非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器；3) 引入自监督学习方法，在无法获取真实健康标签的情况下学习潜在表示；4) 比较无监督表示与直接预测基线的下游估计性能。

Result: 传统滤波器仍然是强基线，自监督学习方法揭示了健康估计的内在复杂性，突显了对更先进和可解释推理策略的需求。研究为健康估计逆问题的难度建立了实用下界。

Conclusion: 该研究通过新数据集和综合基准评估，揭示了涡轮风扇发动机健康估计问题的复杂性。传统方法仍具竞争力，但自监督学习方法展示了在无标签情况下的潜力，强调了开发更先进、可解释推理策略的必要性。数据集和实现代码已开源以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Estimating the health state of turbofan engines is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem, hindered by sparse sensing and complex nonlinear thermodynamics. Research in this area remains fragmented, with comparisons limited by the use of unrealistic datasets and insufficient exploration of the exploitation of temporal information. This work investigates how to recover component-level health indicators from operational sensor data under realistic degradation and maintenance patterns. To support this study, we introduce a new dataset that incorporates industry-oriented complexities such as maintenance events and usage changes. Using this dataset, we establish an initial benchmark that compares steady-state and nonstationary data-driven models, and Bayesian filters, classic families of methods used to solve this problem. In addition to this benchmark, we introduce self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches that learn latent representations without access to true health labels, a scenario reflective of real-world operational constraints. By comparing the downstream estimation performance of these unsupervised representations against the direct prediction baselines, we establish a practical lower bound on the difficulty of solving this inverse problem. Our results reveal that traditional filters remain strong baselines, while SSL methods reveal the intrinsic complexity of health estimation and highlight the need for more advanced and interpretable inference strategies. For reproducibility, both the generated dataset and the implementation used in this work are made accessible.

</details>


### [164] [TTVS: Boosting Self-Exploring Reinforcement Learning via Test-time Variational Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08468)
*Sikai Bai,Haoxi Li,Jie Zhang,Yongjiang Liu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTVS框架通过在线变分合成和测试时混合探索，使大型推理模型能够在无标签测试数据上自我进化，超越传统监督学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习范式在专业或新兴领域存在监督成本过高或不可用的限制，而现有测试时方法容易过拟合静态查询集的文本模式，需要一种能在无标签测试数据上实现自我进化的方法。

Method: 提出测试时变分合成(TTVS)框架，包含两个协同模块：1)在线变分合成模块，将静态测试查询转化为动态的语义等价变体流；2)测试时混合探索模块，平衡基于准确性的利用和基于一致性的探索。

Result: 在八种模型架构上的广泛实验表明，TTVS仅使用无标签测试数据就超越了其他测试时适应方法，甚至优于使用大量高质量标签数据训练的最先进监督RL技术。

Conclusion: TTVS通过动态合成语义等价变体并平衡探索与利用，使大型推理模型能够在无监督测试环境中自我进化，解决了专业领域监督成本高昂的问题，为测试时适应提供了新范式。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) driven by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), this paradigm is fundamentally limited in specialized or novel domains where such supervision is prohibitively expensive or unavailable, posing a key challenge for test-time adaptation. While existing test-time methods offer a potential solution, they are constrained by learning from static query sets, risking overfitting to textual patterns. To address this gap, we introduce Test-Time Variational Synthesis (TTVS), a novel framework that enables LRMs to self-evolve by dynamically augmenting the training stream from unlabeled test queries. TTVS comprises two synergistic modules: (1) Online Variational Synthesis, which transforms static test queries into a dynamic stream of diverse, semantically-equivalent variations, enforcing the model to learn underlying problem logic rather than superficial patterns; (2) Test-time Hybrid Exploration, which balances accuracy-driven exploitation with consistency-driven exploration across synthetic variants. Extensive experiments show TTVS yields superior performance across eight model architectures. Notably, using only unlabeled test-time data, TTVS not only surpasses other test-time adaptation methods but also outperforms state-of-the-art supervised RL-based techniques trained on vast, high-quality labeled data.

</details>


### [165] [Quantization Impact on the Accuracy and Communication Efficiency Trade-off in Federated Learning for Aerospace Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08474)
*Abdelkarim Loukili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了联邦学习中对称均匀量化对航空航天预测性维护模型的影响，发现INT4量化在保持与FP32统计无差异精度的同时，将梯度通信成本降低8倍，而INT2量化则因过度正则化导致不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）在分布式航空航天机队中实现隐私保护的预测性维护，但梯度通信开销限制了其在带宽受限的物联网节点上的部署。需要研究量化技术对精度-效率权衡的影响。

Method: 使用对称均匀量化（b∈{32,8,4,2}位）对自定义轻量级1-D卷积模型（AeroConv1D，9,697参数）进行联邦学习训练，在NASA C-MAPSS基准数据集上采用现实的非独立同分布（Non-IID）客户端分区，并进行多种子严格评估（N=10种子）。

Result: INT4量化在FD001和FD002数据集上达到与FP32统计无差异的精度（p值分别为0.341和0.264/0.534），同时将每轮梯度通信成本从37.88 KiB降低到4.73 KiB（8倍减少）。INT2量化虽然在某些指标上表现较好，但导致NASA评分不稳定（变异系数45.8% vs FP32的22.3%）。

Conclusion: INT4量化是可行的解决方案，能在保持精度的同时显著减少通信开销，适合硬件约束（Xilinx ZCU102上85.5% DSP利用率）。INT2量化因过度正则化导致不稳定，不适合实际部署。研究强调了非独立同分布评估的重要性，揭示了朴素独立同分布分区会人为抑制方差。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving predictive maintenance across distributed aerospace fleets, but gradient communication overhead constrains deployment on bandwidth-limited IoT nodes. This paper investigates the impact of symmetric uniform quantization ($b \in \{32,8,4,2\}$ bits) on the accuracy--efficiency trade-off of a custom-designed lightweight 1-D convolutional model (AeroConv1D, 9\,697 parameters) trained via FL on the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark under a realistic Non-IID client partition. Using a rigorous multi-seed evaluation ($N=10$ seeds), we show that INT4 achieves accuracy \emph{statistically indistinguishable} from FP32 on both FD001 ($p=0.341$) and FD002 ($p=0.264$ MAE, $p=0.534$ NASA score) while delivering an $8\times$ reduction in gradient communication cost (37.88~KiB $\to$ 4.73~KiB per round). A key methodological finding is that naïve IID client partitioning artificially suppresses variance; correct Non-IID evaluation reveals the true operational instability of extreme quantization, demonstrated via a direct empirical IID vs.\ Non-IID comparison. INT2 is empirically characterized as unsuitable: while it achieves lower MAE on FD002 through extreme quantization-induced over-regularization, this apparent gain is accompanied by catastrophic NASA score instability (CV\,=\,45.8\% vs.\ 22.3\% for FP32), confirming non-reproducibility under heterogeneous operating conditions. Analytical FPGA resource projections on the Xilinx ZCU102 confirm that INT4 fits within hardware constraints (85.5\% DSP utilization), potentially enabling a complete FL pipeline on a single SoC. The full simulation codebase and FPGA estimation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/therealdeadbeef/aerospace-fl-quantization.

</details>


### [166] [What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08524)
*Stephen Cheng,Sarah Wiegreffe,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过激活修补框架分析LLM中转向向量的因果机制，发现不同转向方法在相同层使用功能可互换的电路，主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而忽略QK电路。转向向量可稀疏化90-99%仍保持性能，且不同方法在重要维度上存在共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然转向向量是LLM对齐的高效技术，但缺乏对其工作机制的可解释性理解——具体影响哪些内部机制以及如何导致不同模型输出。为探究转向向量有效性的因果机制，研究者以拒绝行为为案例进行深入研究。

Method: 提出多令牌激活修补框架，对拒绝行为进行案例研究。分析不同转向方法在相同层的功能可互换电路，数学分解转向OV电路以揭示语义可解释概念，并实验验证转向向量的稀疏化潜力。

Result: 发现转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而基本忽略QK电路（冻结注意力分数仅使性能下降8.75%）。转向OV电路的数学分解可揭示语义可解释概念，即使转向向量本身不可解释。转向向量可稀疏化90-99%仍保持大部分性能，且不同转向方法在重要维度上存在共识。

Conclusion: 转向向量通过影响LLM中特定电路（主要是OV电路）来改变模型行为，这些电路具有功能可互换性。转向向量的稀疏化潜力表明其作用机制具有高度选择性，不同方法在关键维度上的一致性为理解模型对齐提供了新的可解释性视角。

Abstract: Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Evaluation as Evolution: Transforming Adversarial Diffusion into Closed-Loop Curricula for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07378)
*Yicheng Guo,Jiaqi Liu,Chengkai Xu,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E²框架将对抗性场景生成从静态验证转变为自适应进化课程，通过传输正则化稀疏控制和拓扑驱动支持选择，在保持真实性的同时高效发现安全关键故障案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在交互交通环境中受限于静态数据集中安全关键尾部事件的稀缺性，导致学习策略偏向平均情况行为，降低鲁棒性。现有对抗性压力测试方法多为开环和事后处理，难以将发现的故障案例整合到训练过程中。

Method: 提出E²框架，将对抗性场景合成表述为基于学习到的反向时间SDE先验的传输正则化稀疏控制。采用拓扑驱动支持选择识别关键交互智能体，引入拓扑锚定稳定生成过程，在约束偏离真实数据分布的同时实现目标故障发现。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上碰撞故障发现率提升9.01%，在nuPlan数据集上提升高达21.43%，同时保持低无效性和高真实性。将发现的边界案例用于闭环策略微调可获得显著的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: E²框架成功将对抗性生成从静态验证转变为自适应进化课程，能够在保持现实性的前提下高效发现安全关键故障案例，并通过闭环微调显著提升自动驾驶策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles in interactive traffic environments are often limited by the scarcity of safety-critical tail events in static datasets, which biases learned policies toward average-case behaviors and reduces robustness. Existing evaluation methods attempt to address this through adversarial stress testing, but are predominantly open-loop and post-hoc, making it difficult to incorporate discovered failures back into the training process. We introduce Evaluation as Evolution ($E^2$), a closed-loop framework that transforms adversarial generation from a static validation step into an adaptive evolutionary curriculum. Specifically, $E^2$ formulates adversarial scenario synthesis as transport-regularized sparse control over a learned reverse-time SDE prior. To make this high-dimensional generation tractable, we utilize topology-driven support selection to identify critical interacting agents, and introduce Topological Anchoring to stabilize the process. This approach enables the targeted discovery of failure cases while strictly constraining deviations from realistic data distributions. Empirically, $E^2$ improves collision failure discovery by 9.01% on the nuScenes dataset and up to 21.43% on the nuPlan dataset over the strongest baselines, while maintaining low invalidity and high realism. It further yields substantial robustness gains when the resulting boundary cases are recycled for closed-loop policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [168] [OpenPRC: A Unified Open-Source Framework for Physics-to-Task Evaluation in Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07423)
*Yogesh Phalak,Wen Sin Lor,Apoorva Khairnar,Benjamin Jantzen,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenPRC是一个开源Python框架，为物理储层计算提供统一的物理到任务流水线，支持GPU加速模拟和实验数据，实现可重复的评估、分析和物理感知优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算利用物理基质的固有非线性动力学作为固定计算储层，提供能效高、具身化的机器学习范式。然而，现有工具仅处理流水线的孤立部分，缺乏能够统一表示高保真模拟轨迹和真实实验测量的框架，无法实现跨基质和数据源的可重复评估、分析和物理感知优化。

Method: OpenPRC采用模式驱动的物理到任务流水线，包含五个模块：1) GPU加速的混合RK4-PBD物理引擎(demlat)；2) 基于视频的实验数据摄取层(openprc.vision)；3) 模块化学习层(reservoir)；4) 信息论分析和基准测试工具(analysis)；5) 物理感知优化(optimize)。通用HDF5模式确保可重复性和互操作性。

Result: 展示了折纸镶嵌模拟、物理储层视频轨迹提取、标准化PRC基准测试、相关性诊断和容量分析等能力。框架能够处理GPU模拟和实验获取的轨迹，无需修改即可进入相同下游工作流。

Conclusion: OpenPRC填补了物理储层计算开发与评估工作流的空白，为PRC社区提供标准化层，兼容PyBullet、PyElastica和MERLIN等外部物理引擎，支持跨基质和数据源的可重复研究。

Abstract: Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) leverages the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of physical substrates, mechanical, optical, spintronic, and beyond, as fixed computational reservoirs, offering a compelling paradigm for energy-efficient and embodied machine learning. However, the practical workflow for developing and evaluating PRC systems remains fragmented: existing tools typically address only isolated parts of the pipeline, such as substrate-specific simulation, digital reservoir benchmarking, or readout training. What is missing is a unified framework that can represent both high-fidelity simulated trajectories and real experimental measurements through the same data interface, enabling reproducible evaluation, analysis, and physics-aware optimization across substrates and data sources. We present OpenPRC, an open-source Python framework that fills this gap through a schema-driven physics-to-task pipeline built around five modules: a GPU-accelerated hybrid RK4-PBD physics engine (demlat), a video-based experimental ingestion layer (openprc.vision), a modular learning layer (reservoir), information-theoretic analysis and benchmarking tools (analysis), and physics-aware optimization (optimize). A universal HDF5 schema enforces reproducibility and interoperability, allowing GPU-simulated and experimentally acquired trajectories to enter the same downstream workflow without modification. Demonstrated capabilities include simulations of Origami tessellations, video-based trajectory extraction from a physical reservoir, and a common interface for standardized PRC benchmarking, correlation diagnostics, and capacity analysis. The longer-term vision is to serve as a standardizing layer for the PRC community, compatible with external physics engines including PyBullet, PyElastica, and MERLIN.

</details>


### [169] [CMP: Robust Whole-Body Tracking for Loco-Manipulation via Competence Manifold Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07457)
*Ziyang Cheng,Haoyu Wei,Hang Yin,Xiuwei Xu,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)方法，通过能力流形投影提升腿式移动机械臂对分布外输入的鲁棒性，同时保持任务性能连续性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦控制方案对腿式移动机械臂具有鲁棒性，但学习整体全身控制策略跟踪全局末端执行器位姿时，对传感器噪声或不可行用户命令引起的分布外输入仍很脆弱

Method: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)：1) 使用逐帧安全方案将无限时域安全约束转化为计算高效的单步流形包含；2) 采用下界安全估计器区分训练分布外的未掌握意图；3) 引入同构潜在空间对齐流形几何与安全概率，实现O(1)高效防御

Result: 在典型分布外场景中，CMP实现高达10倍的生存率提升，基线方法出现灾难性失败，同时跟踪性能下降低于10%；系统展现出对分布外目标的"尽力而为"泛化行为

Conclusion: CMP方法有效提升了腿式移动机械臂对分布外输入的鲁棒性，通过能力边界投影实现了安全与性能的平衡，系统能够渐进式完成分布外目标

Abstract: While decoupled control schemes for legged mobile manipulators have shown robustness, learning holistic whole-body control policies for tracking global end-effector poses remains fragile against Out-of-Distribution (OOD) inputs induced by sensor noise or infeasible user commands. To improve robustness against these perturbations without sacrificing task performance and continuity, we propose Competence Manifold Projection (CMP). Specifically, we utilize a Frame-Wise Safety Scheme that transforms the infinite-horizon safety constraint into a computationally efficient single-step manifold inclusion. To instantiate this competence manifold, we employ a Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator that distinguishes unmastered intentions from the training distribution. We then introduce an Isomorphic Latent Space (ILS) that aligns manifold geometry with safety probability, enabling efficient O(1) seamless defense against arbitrary OOD intents. Experiments demonstrate that CMP achieves up to a 10-fold survival rate improvement in typical OOD scenarios where baselines suffer catastrophic failure, incurring under 10% tracking degradation. Notably, the system exhibits emergent ``best-effort'' generalization behaviors to progressively accomplish OOD goals by adhering to the competence boundaries. Result videos are available at: https://shepherd1226.github.io/CMP.

</details>


### [170] [Active Reward Machine Inference From Raw State Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07480)
*Mohamad Louai Shehab,Antoine Aspeel,Necmiye Ozay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种从原始状态和策略信息中学习奖励机的方法，无需访问奖励、标签或机器节点观测，并扩展到主动学习以提高数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动指定奖励机（包括捕捉决策基础的高层特征的标注函数）是一项艰巨任务。现有方法通常需要访问奖励、标签或机器节点观测，但在信息稀缺的情况下学习奖励机是一个挑战。

Method: 提出从原始状态和策略信息直接学习奖励机的方法，不依赖奖励、标签或机器节点观测。确定在信息稀缺情况下学习奖励机所需的轨迹数据条件。进一步扩展到主动学习设置，通过增量查询轨迹扩展来提高数据和计算效率。

Result: 在多个网格世界示例中验证了方法的有效性，展示了在信息稀缺情况下学习奖励机的可行性，以及主动学习在提高数据效率方面的优势。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在无需奖励、标签或机器节点观测的情况下学习奖励机，为机器人策略合成提供了更实用的解决方案。主动学习扩展进一步提高了数据效率，使奖励机学习在现实应用中更加可行。

Abstract: Reward machines are automaton-like structures that capture the memory required to accomplish a multi-stage task. When combined with reinforcement learning or optimal control methods, they can be used to synthesize robot policies to achieve such tasks. However, specifying a reward machine by hand, including a labeling function capturing high-level features that the decisions are based on, can be a daunting task. This paper deals with the problem of learning reward machines directly from raw state and policy information. As opposed to existing works, we assume no access to observations of rewards, labels, or machine nodes, and show what trajectory data is sufficient for learning the reward machine in this information-scarce regime. We then extend the result to an active learning setting where we incrementally query trajectory extensions to improve data (and indirectly computational) efficiency. Results are demonstrated with several grid world examples.

</details>


### [171] [Grasp as You Dream: Imitating Functional Grasping from Generated Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07517)
*Chao Tang,Jiacheng Xu,Haofei Lu,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Hong Zhang,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspDreamer：利用视觉生成模型合成人类演示数据，实现零样本功能抓取，无需大规模数据收集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能在开放世界环境中执行功能抓取的通用机器人面临巨大挑战，现有方法要么局限于狭窄的对象/任务集，要么依赖大规模数据收集来捕捉现实世界变化性

Method: 利用预训练在互联网规模人类数据上的视觉生成模型（如视频生成模型）合成人类演示，这些模型隐式编码了人类与物理世界交互的通用先验知识，再结合特定具身化的动作优化来实现功能抓取

Result: 在不同机器人手上的公共基准测试中，GraspDreamer相比先前方法展现出优越的数据效率和泛化性能；真实世界评估进一步验证了其在真实机器人上的有效性；该方法还能自然扩展到下游操作任务，并生成数据支持视觉运动策略学习

Conclusion: GraspDreamer提供了一种替代方法，利用视觉生成模型合成的人类演示实现零样本功能抓取，避免了劳动密集型数据收集，展示了优越的数据效率和泛化能力

Abstract: Building generalist robots capable of performing functional grasping in everyday, open-world environments remains a significant challenge due to the vast diversity of objects and tasks. Existing methods are either constrained to narrow object/task sets or rely on prohibitively large-scale data collection to capture real-world variability. In this work, we present an alternative approach, GraspDreamer, a method that leverages human demonstrations synthesized by visual generative models (VGMs) (e.g., video generation models) to enable zero-shot functional grasping without labor-intensive data collection. The key idea is that VGMs pre-trained on internet-scale human data implicitly encode generalized priors about how humans interact with the physical world, which can be combined with embodiment-specific action optimization to enable functional grasping with minimal effort. Extensive experiments on the public benchmarks with different robot hands demonstrate the superior data efficiency and generalization performance of GraspDreamer compared to previous methods. Real-world evaluations further validate the effectiveness on real robots. Additionally, we showcase that GraspDreamer can (1) be naturally extended to downstream manipulation tasks, and (2) can generate data to support visuomotor policy learning.

</details>


### [172] [Spatio-Temporal Grounding of Large Language Models from Perception Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07592)
*Jacob Anderson,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Hideki Okamoto,Danil Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FESTS框架通过将自然语言查询编译为空间正则表达式，为LLM提供可验证的时空监督，无需人工标注即可生成无限训练数据，使3B参数模型在时空推理上达到GPT-4.1水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小型前沿LLM在处理细粒度空间关系、度量距离和时间顺序方面存在不足，而具身AI智能体需要在3D空间和时间维度上推理物体的运动和交互。

Method: 提出FESTS框架，将自然语言查询编译为空间正则表达式（结合正则表达式语法与S4u空间逻辑，并扩展了全称和存在量化），匹配结构化视频日志生成对齐的（查询、帧、匹配、解释）元组作为训练数据。

Result: 在27k个元组上训练3B参数模型，帧级F1从48.5%提升到87.5%，在复杂时空推理上匹配GPT-4.1性能，同时模型规模小两个数量级。

Conclusion: FESTS框架通过可验证的时空监督显著提升了小型LLM的时空推理能力，为视频LLM实现时空智能提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Embodied-AI agents must reason about how objects move and interact in 3-D space over time, yet existing smaller frontier Large Language Models (LLMs) still mis-handle fine-grained spatial relations, metric distances, and temporal orderings. We introduce the general framework Formally Explainable Spatio-Temporal Scenes (FESTS) that injects verifiable spatio-temporal supervision into an LLM by compiling natural-language queries into Spatial Regular Expression (SpRE) -- a language combining regular expression syntax with S4u spatial logic and extended here with universal and existential quantification. The pipeline matches each SpRE against any structured video log and exports aligned (query, frames, match, explanation) tuples, enabling unlimited training data without manual labels. Training a 3-billion-parameter model on 27k such tuples boosts frame-level F1 from 48.5% to 87.5%, matching GPT-4.1 on complex spatio-temporal reasoning while remaining two orders of magnitude smaller, and, hence, enabling spatio-temporal intelligence for Video LLM.

</details>


### [173] [SANDO: Safe Autonomous Trajectory Planning for Dynamic Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07599)
*Kota Kondo,Jesús Tordesillas,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SANDO是一个用于3D动态未知环境的安全轨迹规划器，通过热图A*全局规划、时空安全飞行走廊生成和混合整数二次规划优化，在保证安全的同时实现快速重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D动态未知环境中，障碍物位置和运动事先未知，且无碰撞路径可能随时变得不安全，需要快速重规划。现有软约束规划器速度快但不能保证无碰撞路径，而硬约束方法虽能确保安全但计算时间长。SANDO旨在解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 1. 热图A*全局规划器使用软成本引导路径远离高风险区域；时空安全飞行走廊生成器产生时间分层多面体，仅根据每个时间层的最坏可达集膨胀障碍物。2. 将轨迹优化公式化为混合整数二次规划，采用变量消除技术减少决策变量。3. 形式化安全分析在明确速度边界和估计误差假设下建立无碰撞保证。

Result: 变量消除技术使优化时间加速高达7.4倍；时空安全飞行走廊在密集动态环境中对可行性至关重要。在标准化静态基准、障碍物丰富的静态森林和动态环境的基准测试中，SANDO在所有难度级别上始终实现最高成功率且无约束违反；仅感知实验在无地面真实障碍物信息下也表现出稳健性能。硬件实验在无人机上实现6次静态环境安全飞行和10次动态障碍物安全飞行。

Conclusion: SANDO通过结合软约束全局规划和硬约束局部优化，在3D动态未知环境中实现了安全与计算效率的平衡，为无人机在复杂动态环境中的自主导航提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: SANDO is a safe trajectory planner for 3D dynamic unknown environments, where obstacle locations and motions are unknown a priori and a collision-free plan can become unsafe at any moment, requiring fast replanning. Existing soft-constraint planners are fast but cannot guarantee collision-free paths, while hard-constraint methods ensure safety at the cost of longer computation. SANDO addresses this trade-off through three contributions. First, a heat map-based A* global planner steers paths away from high-risk regions using soft costs, and a spatiotemporal safe flight corridor (STSFC) generator produces time-layered polytopes that inflate obstacles only by their worst-case reachable set at each time layer, rather than by the worst case over the entire horizon. Second, trajectory optimization is formulated as a Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) with hard collision-avoidance constraints, and a variable elimination technique reduces the number of decision variables, enabling fast computation. Third, a formal safety analysis establishes collision-free guarantees under explicit velocity-bound and estimation-error assumptions. Ablation studies show that variable elimination yields up to 7.4x speedup in optimization time, and that STSFCs are critical for feasibility in dense dynamic environments. Benchmark simulations against state-of-the-art methods across standardized static benchmarks, obstacle-rich static forests, and dynamic environments show that SANDO consistently achieves the highest success rate with no constraint violations across all difficulty levels; perception-only experiments without ground truth obstacle information confirm robust performance under realistic sensing. Hardware experiments on a UAV with fully onboard planning, perception, and localization demonstrate six safe flights in static environments and ten safe flights among dynamic obstacles.

</details>


### [174] [EgoVerse: An Egocentric Human Dataset for Robot Learning from Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07607)
*Ryan Punamiya,Simar Kareer,Zeyi Liu,Josh Citron,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Xiongyi Cai,Alexey Gavryushin,Jiaqi Chen,Davide Liconti,Lawrence Y. Zhu,Patcharapong Aphiwetsa,Baoyu Li,Aniketh Cheluva,Pranav Kuppili,Yangcen Liu,Dhruv Patel,Aidan Gao,Hye-Young Chung,Ryan Co,Renee Zbizika,Jeff Liu,Xiaomeng Xu,Haoyu Xiong,Geng Chen,Sebastiano Oliani,Chenyu Yang,Xi Wang,James Fort,Richard Newcombe,Josh Gao,Jason Chong,Garrett Matsuda,Aseem Doriwala,Marc Pollefeys,Robert Katzschmann,Xiaolong Wang,Shuran Song,Judy Hoffman,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoVerse是一个用于机器人学习的协作平台，统一了人类数据收集、处理和访问，包含1,362小时人类演示数据，并通过多实验室研究验证了人类数据对机器人策略性能的积极影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模多样化数据，但机器人数据收集成本高且难以扩展。人类自我中心数据提供了丰富的日常操作行为，但现有数据集范围有限、难以扩展且分散在不同机构。

Method: 开发EgoVerse协作平台，统一数据收集、处理和访问框架；收集1,362小时人类演示数据，涵盖1,965个任务、240个场景、2,087名演示者；建立标准化格式、操作相关标注和下游学习工具；进行多实验室、多任务、多机器人形态的人类到机器人转移研究。

Result: 当前版本包含1,362小时（80k片段）人类演示数据；大规模研究表明策略性能通常随人类数据增加而提升，但有效扩展取决于人类数据与机器人学习目标的对齐程度。

Conclusion: EgoVerse的数据集、平台和研究为人类数据驱动的机器人学习建立了可重复进展的基础，展示了协作数据收集和标准化协议对推动领域发展的重要性。

Abstract: Robot learning increasingly depends on large and diverse data, yet robot data collection remains expensive and difficult to scale. Egocentric human data offer a promising alternative by capturing rich manipulation behavior across everyday environments. However, existing human datasets are often limited in scope, difficult to extend, and fragmented across institutions. We introduce EgoVerse, a collaborative platform for human data-driven robot learning that unifies data collection, processing, and access under a shared framework, enabling contributions from individual researchers, academic labs, and industry partners. The current release includes 1,362 hours (80k episodes) of human demonstrations spanning 1,965 tasks, 240 scenes, and 2,087 unique demonstrators, with standardized formats, manipulation-relevant annotations, and tooling for downstream learning. Beyond the dataset, we conduct a large-scale study of human-to-robot transfer with experiments replicated across multiple labs, tasks, and robot embodiments under shared protocols. We find that policy performance generally improves with increased human data, but that effective scaling depends on alignment between human data and robot learning objectives. Together, the dataset, platform, and study establish a foundation for reproducible progress in human data-driven robot learning. Videos and additional information can be found at https://egoverse.ai/

</details>


### [175] [Safe Large-Scale Robust Nonlinear MPC in Milliseconds via Reachability-Constrained System Level Synthesis on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07644)
*Jeffrey Fang,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPU-SLS：一个GPU并行化的安全鲁棒非线性模型预测控制框架，适用于高维不确定机器人系统和长规划时域，实现实时联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在高维不确定机器人系统和长规划时域下实现实时、安全的非线性模型预测控制。传统CPU求解器计算效率低，而现有GPU求解器仍有优化空间，且高维学习型可达性方法无法保证100%安全性。

Method: 开发GPU并行化框架，采用顺序二次规划方法，结合新型GPU加速二次规划求解器（使用并行关联扫描和自适应缓存的ADMM框架）。使用相同GPU QP后端通过系统级合成优化鲁棒跟踪控制器和闭环可达集，支持固定和滚动时域下的可达性约束控制。

Result: 相比最先进CPU求解器，名义轨迹求解时间减少97.7%；相比GPU求解器减少71.8%；SLS控制和可达性计算加速237倍。在复杂非线性系统（四足机器人61D、人形机器人75D）上验证，平均20毫秒在线合成鲁棒控制策略，可处理2×10^5决策变量和8×10^4约束问题，实现100%经验安全性。

Conclusion: GPU-SLS框架显著提升了高维不确定机器人系统非线性模型预测控制的实时性和安全性，为复杂机器人系统提供了可扩展的实时鲁棒控制解决方案。

Abstract: We present GPU-SLS, a GPU-parallelized framework for safe, robust nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that scales to high-dimensional uncertain robotic systems and long planning horizons. Our method jointly optimizes an inequality-constrained, dynamically-feasible nominal trajectory, a tracking controller, and a closed-loop reachable set under disturbance, all in real-time. To efficiently compute nominal trajectories, we develop a sequential quadratic programming procedure with a novel GPU-accelerated quadratic program (QP) solver that uses parallel associative scans and adaptive caching within an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework. The same GPU QP backend is used to optimize robust tracking controllers and closed-loop reachable sets via system level synthesis (SLS), enabling reachability-constrained control in both fixed- and receding-horizon settings. We achieve substantial performance gains, reducing nominal trajectory solve times by 97.7% relative to state-of-the-art CPU solvers and 71.8% compared to GPU solvers, while accelerating SLS-based control and reachability by 237x. Despite large problem scales, our method achieves 100% empirical safety, unlike high-dimensional learning-based reachability baselines. We validate our approach on complex nonlinear systems, including whole-body quadrupeds (61D) and humanoids (75D), synthesizing robust control policies online on the GPU in 20 milliseconds on average and scaling to problems with 2 x 10^5 decision variables and 8 x 10^4 constraints. The implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/Jeff300fang/gpu_sls.

</details>


### [176] [Reset-Free Reinforcement Learning for Real-World Agile Driving: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07672)
*Kohei Honda,Hirotaka Hosogaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过实证研究探索了无重置强化学习在真实世界敏捷驾驶中的应用，发现仿真与真实世界存在显著差距，TD-MPC2在物理平台上表现最佳，而残差学习在真实世界中未能有效迁移其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决高速极限驾驶场景下强化学习面临的挑战：复杂车辆动力学、执行延迟和未建模效应阻碍了精确仿真和直接仿真到真实的策略迁移。需要在物理平台上实现自主训练，避免人工重置。

Method: 采用模型预测路径积分控制作为重置策略和残差学习的基础策略，系统比较了PPO、SAC和TD-MPC2三种代表性RL算法在仿真和真实实验中的表现，包括使用和不使用残差学习的情况。

Result: 仿真与真实世界存在明显差距：SAC+残差学习在仿真中表现最佳，但只有TD-MPC2在物理平台上持续优于MPPI基线。残差学习在仿真中明显有益，但在真实世界中未能迁移其优势，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 真实世界的无重置RL面临仿真中不存在的独特挑战，需要针对野外训练环境开发专门的算法。TD-MPC2在物理平台上的稳定表现表明模型预测方法在真实世界RL中具有潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical study of reset-free reinforcement learning (RL) for real-world agile driving, in which a physical 1/10-scale vehicle learns continuously on a slippery indoor track without manual resets. High-speed driving near the limits of tire friction is particularly challenging for learning-based methods because complex vehicle dynamics, actuation delays, and other unmodeled effects hinder both accurate simulation and direct sim-to-real transfer of learned policies. To enable autonomous training on a physical platform, we employ Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) as both the reset policy and the base policy for residual learning, and systematically compare three representative RL algorithms, i.e., PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2, with and without residual learning in simulation and real-world experiments. Our results reveal a clear gap between simulation and real-world: SAC with residual learning achieves the highest returns in simulation, yet only TD-MPC2 consistently outperforms the MPPI baseline on the physical platform. Moreover, residual learning, while clearly beneficial in simulation, fails to transfer its advantage to the real world and can even degrade performance. These findings reveal that reset-free RL in the real world poses unique challenges absent from simulation, calling for further algorithmic development tailored to training in the wild.

</details>


### [177] [Bird-Inspired Spatial Flapping Wing Mechanism via Coupled Linkages with Single Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07677)
*Daniel Huczala,Sun-Pill Jung,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于耦合空间四杆机构的鸟类仿生扑翼机构，采用单电机驱动，通过被动状态切换实现协调的扫掠-折叠翼运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间单环机构如Bennett机构具有单自由度驱动和非平凡空间轨迹的优点，适合轻量化仿生机器人设计。然而，其几何任务综合因数学复杂性而复杂，在工程任务中常被回避。

Method: 提出一种由两个耦合空间四杆机构组成的鸟类仿生扑翼机构，一个机构主动驱动产生所需空间扫掠行程，另一个串联耦合机构无驱动，在行程周期中被动切换伸展和折叠翼配置。引入简化的运动学方法，从包含所需表面积的四边形构建Bennett机构，并利用机械诱导的被动状态切换。

Result: 该架构实现了单驱动输入的协调扫掠-折叠翼运动，减轻了重量和控制复杂性。3D打印原型组装测试，展示了预期的空间行程和被动折叠行为。

Conclusion: 通过简化Bennett机构的运动学综合方法，结合被动状态切换机制，成功实现了单驱动仿生扑翼机构，为轻量化生物启发机器人设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial single-loop mechanisms such as Bennett linkages offer a unique combination of one-degree-of-freedom actuation and nontrivial spatial trajectories, making them attractive for lightweight bio-inspired robotic design. However, although they appear simple and elegant, the geometric task-based synthesis is rather complicated and often avoided in engineering tasks due to the mathematical complexity involved. This paper presents a bird-inspired flapping-wing mechanism built from two coupled spatial four-bars, driven by a single motor. One linkage is actuated to generate the desired spatial sweeping stroke, while the serially coupled linkage remains unactuated and passively switches between extended and folded wing configurations over the stroke cycle. We introduce a simplified kinematic methodology for constructing Bennett linkages from quadrilaterals that contain a desired surface area and further leverage mechanically induced passive state switching. This architecture realizes a coordinated sweep-and-fold wing motion with a single actuation input, reducing weight and control complexity. A 3D-printed prototype is assembled and tested, demonstrating the intended spatial stroke and passive folding behavior.

</details>


### [178] [Vision-Language Navigation for Aerial Robots: Towards the Era of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07705)
*Xingyu Xia,Lekai Zhou,Yujie Tang,Xiaozhou Zhu,Hai Zhu,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对空中视觉语言导航领域进行了系统性综述，重点关注大语言模型和视觉语言模型的集成，提出了分类框架并分析了关键挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机技术的快速发展，需要让无人机能够理解自然语言指令并在复杂三维环境中自主导航。现有研究分散且缺乏系统性分析，特别是LLM/VLM集成后的新方法需要整理和评估。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法：1) 正式定义Aerial VLN问题及两种交互范式；2) 将方法分为五类架构进行技术分析；3) 评估基础设施（数据集、平台、指标）；4) 跨方法比较分析架构权衡；5) 识别七个开放性问题。

Result: 建立了Aerial VLN方法的五类分类法，分析了各类设计原理和技术权衡，评估了现有基础设施的局限性，比较了不同方法在共享基准上的性能，识别了关键架构权衡点。

Conclusion: Aerial VLN领域在LLM/VLM集成后取得进展但仍面临重大挑战，需要解决长时指令接地、视角鲁棒性、可扩展空间表示、连续6-DoF动作执行、机载部署、基准标准化和多无人机集群导航等七个开放性问题。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (Aerial VLN) aims to enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to interpret natural language instructions and autonomously navigate complex three-dimensional environments by grounding language in visual perception. This survey provides a critical and analytical review of the Aerial VLN field, with particular attention to the recent integration of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). We first formally introduce the Aerial VLN problem and define two interaction paradigms: single-instruction and dialog-based, as foundational axes. We then organize the body of Aerial VLN methods into a taxonomy of five architectural categories: sequence-to-sequence and attention-based methods, end-to-end LLM/VLM methods, hierarchical methods, multi-agent methods, and dialog-based navigation methods. For each category, we systematically analyze design rationales, technical trade-offs, and reported performance. We critically assess the evaluation infrastructure for Aerial VLN, including datasets, simulation platforms, and metrics, and identify their gaps in scale, environmental diversity, real-world grounding, and metric coverage. We consolidate cross-method comparisons on shared benchmarks and analyze key architectural trade-offs, including discrete versus continuous actions, end-to-end versus hierarchical designs, and the simulation-to-reality gap. Finally, we synthesize seven concrete open problems: long-horizon instruction grounding, viewpoint robustness, scalable spatial representation, continuous 6-DoF action execution, onboard deployment, benchmark standardization, and multi-UAV swarm navigation, with specific research directions grounded in the evidence presented throughout the survey.

</details>


### [179] [RoboAgent: Chaining Basic Capabilities for Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07774)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAgent：一种基于能力驱动的具身任务规划框架，通过调度器调用不同子能力来分解复杂规划问题，使视觉语言模型能更好地处理多轮交互、长程推理和扩展上下文分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在具身任务规划中表现有限，难以处理多轮交互、长程推理和扩展上下文分析。需要一种方法将复杂规划分解为VLM能更好处理的基本视觉语言问题。

Method: 提出能力驱动的规划管道，模型主动调用不同子能力，每个能力维护自身上下文。调度器和所有能力由单一VLM实现。采用三阶段训练范式：行为克隆、DAgger训练和强化学习，利用环境模拟器内部信息构建高质量监督，并引入增强和合成数据。

Result: 在广泛使用的具身任务规划基准测试中进行了大量实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。代码将在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: RoboAgent框架通过能力分解使VLM能更好地处理复杂具身规划任务，实现了更透明可控的推理过程，为具身智能提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on embodied task planning, where an agent acquires visual observations from the environment and executes atomic actions to accomplish a given task. Although recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results in multimodal understanding and reasoning, their performance remains limited when applied to embodied planning that involves multi-turn interaction, long-horizon reasoning, and extended context analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose RoboAgent, a capability-driven planning pipeline in which the model actively invokes different sub-capabilities. Each capability maintains its own context, and produces intermediate reasoning results or interacts with the environment according to the query given by a scheduler. This framework decomposes complex planning into a sequence of basic vision-language problems that VLMs can better address, enabling a more transparent and controllable reasoning process. The scheduler and all capabilities are implemented with a single VLM, without relying on external tools. To train this VLM, we adopt a multi-stage paradigm that consists of: (1) behavior cloning with expert plans, (2) DAgger training using trajectories collected by the model, and (3) reinforcement learning guided by an expert policy. Across these stages, we exploit the internal information of the environment simulator to construct high-quality supervision for each capability, and we further introduce augmented and synthetic data to enhance the model's performance in more diverse scenarios. Extensive experiments on widely used embodied task planning benchmarks validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Our codes will be available at https://github.com/woyut/RoboAgent_CVPR26.

</details>


### [180] [Learning Without Losing Identity: Capability Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07799)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种能力中心化的演化范式，将具身智能体的身份与能力演化分离，通过模块化、版本化的具身能力模块实现持续改进，同时保持智能体身份的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过修改智能体本身（如提示工程、策略更新或结构重设计）来提升性能，这会导致长期系统中智能体不稳定和身份丢失。需要一种能够在动态物理环境中持续运行并获取新能力，同时保持智能体身份稳定的方法。

Method: 引入具身能力模块（ECMs）作为模块化、版本化的具身功能单元，将能力演化与智能体身份解耦。通过任务执行、经验收集、模型精炼和模块更新的闭环过程实现能力演化，所有执行由运行时层管理以确保安全和策略约束。

Result: 在模拟具身任务中，能力演化在20次迭代后将任务成功率从32.4%提升至91.3%，优于智能体修改基线和现有技能学习方法（SPiRL、SkiMo），同时保持零策略漂移和零安全违规。

Conclusion: 将智能体身份与能力演化分离为长期具身智能提供了可扩展且安全的基础，使智能体能够在保持认知身份的同时持续改进能力。

Abstract: Embodied agents are expected to operate persistently in dynamic physical environments, continuously acquiring new capabilities over time. Existing approaches to improving agent performance often rely on modifying the agent itself -- through prompt engineering, policy updates, or structural redesign -- leading to instability and loss of identity in long-lived systems. In this work, we propose a capability-centric evolution paradigm for embodied agents. We argue that a robot should maintain a persistent agent as its cognitive identity, while enabling continuous improvement through the evolution of its capabilities. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Embodied Capability Modules (ECMs), which represent modular, versioned units of embodied functionality that can be learned, refined, and composed over time. We present a unified framework in which capability evolution is decoupled from agent identity. Capabilities evolve through a closed-loop process involving task execution, experience collection, model refinement, and module updating, while all executions are governed by a runtime layer that enforces safety and policy constraints. We demonstrate through simulated embodied tasks that capability evolution improves task success rates from 32.4% to 91.3% over 20 iterations, outperforming both agent-modification baselines and established skill-learning methods (SPiRL, SkiMo), while preserving zero policy drift and zero safety violations. Our results suggest that separating agent identity from capability evolution provides a scalable and safe foundation for long-term embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [181] [Harnessing Embodied Agents: Runtime Governance for Policy-Constrained Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07833)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于具身智能体的运行时治理框架，将认知与执行监督分离，通过外部化治理层实现策略检查、能力准入、执行监控等功能，显著提升动作安全性和合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身智能体从被动推理系统演变为与工具、机器人和物理环境交互的主动执行器，一旦授予执行权限，核心挑战在于如何在运行时保持动作的可治理性。现有方法将安全和恢复逻辑嵌入智能体循环内部，使得执行控制难以标准化、审计和适应。

Method: 提出策略约束执行框架，将智能体认知与执行监督分离。治理被外部化为专门的运行时层，执行策略检查、能力准入、执行监控、回滚处理和人工覆盖。形式化定义了具身智能体、具身能力模块和运行时治理层之间的控制边界。

Result: 通过1000次随机模拟试验验证，结果显示：96.2%的未授权动作被拦截；在运行时漂移情况下，不安全延续从100%降至22.2%；91.4%的恢复成功率且完全符合策略要求，显著优于所有基线方法（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 通过将运行时治理重构为一类系统问题，本文提出策略约束执行应作为具身智能体系统的关键设计原则，强调需要更强的运行时治理而不仅仅是更强的智能体。

Abstract: Embodied agents are evolving from passive reasoning systems into active executors that interact with tools, robots, and physical environments. Once granted execution authority, the central challenge becomes how to keep actions governable at runtime. Existing approaches embed safety and recovery logic inside the agent loop, making execution control difficult to standardize, audit, and adapt.
  This paper argues that embodied intelligence requires not only stronger agents, but stronger runtime governance. We propose a framework for policy-constrained execution that separates agent cognition from execution oversight. Governance is externalized into a dedicated runtime layer performing policy checking, capability admission, execution monitoring, rollback handling, and human override.
  We formalize the control boundary among the embodied agent, Embodied Capability Modules (ECMs), and runtime governance layer, and validate through 1000 randomized simulation trials across three governance dimensions. Results show 96.2% interception of unauthorized actions, reduction of unsafe continuation from 100% to 22.2% under runtime drift, and 91.4% recovery success with full policy compliance, substantially outperforming all baselines (p<0.001). By reframing runtime governance as a first-class systems problem, this paper positions policy-constrained execution as a key design principle for embodied agent systems.

</details>


### [182] [The Sustainability Gap in Robotics: A Large-Scale Survey of Sustainability Awareness in 50,000 Research Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07921)
*Antun Skuric,Leandro Von Werra,Thomas Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对arXiv cs.RO类别近5万篇论文的大规模调查显示，机器人学研究中的可持续性沟通与动机存在显著差距：尽管许多研究可映射到SDG相关领域，但明确提及可持续性影响的比例低于2%，SDG引用低于0.1%，可持续性动机论文不足5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在量化机器人学研究领域对可持续性沟通和动机的关注程度，评估该领域研究与社会、生态及可持续性影响的关联性，以及其与联合国可持续发展目标（SDGs）的对齐情况，揭示机器人学研究的潜在社会影响与实际表述意图之间的差距。

Method: 对arXiv cs.RO类别2015年至2026年初发表的近50,000篇开放获取论文进行大规模文本分析，量化论文中提及社会、生态和可持续性影响的频率，分析这些内容与联合国可持续发展目标（SDGs）的对齐程度。

Result: 结果显示：可持续性相关影响提及率通常低于2%，明确SDG引用低于0.1%，具有可持续性动机的论文比例低于5%。虽然大量机器人学论文可映射到SDG相关领域，但明确的可持续性动机仍然极低，表明该领域在快速发展的同时，可持续性尚未成为研究框架的标准组成部分。

Conclusion: 机器人学研究领域存在显著的可持续性意识与动机差距，需要研究人员、会议和机构采取具体行动来弥合这些差距，推动向更有意识和负责任的创新转变。

Abstract: We present a large-scale survey of sustainability communication and motivation in robotics research. Our analysis covers nearly 50,000 open-access papers from arXiv's cs.RO category published between 2015 and early 2026. In this study, we quantify how often papers mention social, ecological, and sustainability impacts, and we analyse their alignment with the UN Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).
  The results reveal a persistent gap between the field's potential and its stated intent. While a large fraction of robotics papers can be mapped to SDG-relevant domains, explicit sustainability motivation remains remarkably low. Specifically, mentions of sustainability-related impacts are typically below 2%, explicit SDG references stay below 0.1%, and the proportion of sustainability-motivated papers remains below 5%. These trends suggest that while the field of robotics is advancing rapidly, sustainability is not yet a standard part of research framing.
  We conclude by proposing concrete actions for researchers, conferences, and institutions to close these awareness and motivation gaps, supporting a shift toward more intentional and responsible innovation.

</details>


### [183] [RAGE-XY: RADAR-Aided Longitudinal and Lateral Forces Estimation For Autonomous Race Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07939)
*Davide Malvezzi,Nicola Musiu,Eugenio Mascaro,Francesco Iacovacci,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGE-XY扩展了RAGE实时估计框架，通过在线RADAR校准模块和三轮车模型，提高了车辆横向速度估计精度并实现了后轮纵向轮胎力的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始RAGE框架在传感器未对准时横向速度估计精度受限，且仅能估计横向动力学。需要改进传感器校准并扩展模型以同时估计纵向轮胎力。

Method: 1) 引入在线RADAR校准模块处理传感器未对准问题；2) 将基础车辆模型从单轨近似扩展到三轮车模型；3) 仅使用标准车载传感器（IMU和RADAR）实时估计车辆速度、轮胎滑移角和车辆受力。

Result: 在高保真仿真和EAV-24自动驾驶赛车真实实验中验证，RAGE-XY在横向和纵向车辆动力学估计方面表现出更高的精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RAGE-XY通过在线传感器校准和扩展的车辆模型，显著提高了车辆状态估计性能，为自动驾驶系统提供了更准确的动力学信息。

Abstract: In this work, we present RAGE-XY, an extended version of RAGE, a real-time estimation framework that simultaneously infers vehicle velocity, tire slip angles, and the forces acting on the vehicle using only standard onboard sensors such as IMUs and RADARs. Compared to the original formulation, the proposed method incorporates an online RADAR calibration module, improving the accuracy of lateral velocity estimation in the presence of sensor misalignment. Furthermore, we extend the underlying vehicle model from a single-track approximation to a tricycle model, enabling the estimation of rear longitudinal tire forces in addition to lateral dynamics. We validate the proposed approach through both high-fidelity simulations and real-world experiments conducted on the EAV-24 autonomous race car, demonstrating improved accuracy and robustness in estimating both lateral and longitudinal vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [184] [On-Policy Distillation of Language Models for Autonomous Vehicle Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07944)
*Amirhossein Afsharrad,Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Sanjay Lall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于广义知识蒸馏（GKD）的方法，将大型语言模型在自动驾驶运动规划中的知识迁移到更小的可部署学生模型中，在模型尺寸减少5倍的情况下接近教师模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动驾驶运动规划中展现出潜力，但将其部署到资源受限的车载系统面临挑战。需要研究如何将大型教师LLM的运动规划知识有效迁移到更小、更可部署的学生模型中。
